Evaluations on the TCGA dataset demonstrate that M$^3$-Gen generates realistic and functionally meaningful gene expression data. Importantly, by integrating multiple modalities in an attention-based mechanism, M$^3$-Gen provides intrinsic explainability: it allows the identification of which regions of the histopathology images most strongly influenced the generation of specific gene expression profiles, making the model's decisions interpretable by design.

</details>


### [95] [How Many Bits Can an Adapter Write? Measuring the Capacity and Memorization of Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21351)
*Kaizhen Tan,Heqing Du,Yang Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了直接测量低秩适配器（LoRA）写入模型的信息量（以比特为单位）的方法，发现适配器的记忆容量取决于参数位置而非数量，并且信息写入量与隐私泄露直接相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将LoRA适配器简单地视为技能模块，但缺乏对其实际记忆信息量的直接测量。研究需要从信息论角度深入理解参数高效微调方法的记忆机制，为隐私风险和模型设计提供精确的量化依据。

Method: 将基于压缩的记忆分析扩展到冻结基座设置，通过直接测量适配器写入不变模型的比特数来量化记忆容量。系统性地测试了不同参数位置（注意力层vs MLP层）、基座结构完整性、监督学习与强化学习等多种设置下的信息写入量。

Result: 适配器的记忆容量取决于参数位置而非数量：相同参数预算从注意力层移至MLP层可存储近两倍信息；基座结构被破坏则容量几乎消失。监督微调会逐字记录训练数据中的隐私信息，而基于可验证奖励的强化学习适配器不记录隐私。隐私泄露程度与比特写入量而非名义参数数量相关。

Conclusion: 通过测量微调写入的信息量而非事后攻击评估，将经验性认知转化为可量化、可设计优化的指标。这为理解参数高效微调的记忆机制和隐私风险提供了精确的分析工具，并揭示了监督学习与强化学习在隐私保护方面的本质差异。

Abstract: A LoRA adapter is a few megabytes that almost everyone treats as a skill rather than a record of the data behind it. We put that assumption on a scale. Extending compression-based memorization analysis to the frozen-base setting, we measure directly, in bits, how much a low-rank adapter writes into a model it never changes. The answer is both smaller than full fine-tuning and less lawful than parameter counting would predict. Adapters store a couple of bits per trainable parameter, well short of a full model's budget, but that figure turns less on how many parameters an adapter carries than on where they sit. Move the same parameter budget from attention into the MLP and it holds nearly twice as much; strip the frozen base of its structure and the capacity all but disappears. Applied to realistic fine-tunes of Qwen2.5, the same instrument shows privacy leakage rising with the bits an adapter writes rather than the parameters it nominally has, and it draws a clean line between supervised and reinforcement learning: the secrets that supervised fine-tuning copies down verbatim, an adapter trained on verifiable rewards never records. Measuring what fine-tuning writes, rather than attacking it after the fact, turns a piece of folklore into a quantity one can design against.

</details>


### [96] [Gradient Concentration, Not Weight Saliency, Explains Representation-Level Class Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21353)
*Billel Habbati,Alessio Merlo,Luca Verderame,Meriem Guerar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次通过对照实验发现，在机器遗忘领域广泛使用的基于梯度的显著性参数选择机制，在表示层面的遗忘效果上与随机掩码无显著差异，表明当前显著性权重选择策略作用有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多先进的机器遗忘方法采用基于梯度的显著性参数选择机制，但该机制在表示层面遗忘中的实际贡献尚不清楚，缺乏对其有效性的系统性评估。

Method: 在CIFAR-10和CIFAR-100数据集上使用ResNet-18，采用匹配计算量的实验设计，对比了SalUn的显著性掩码、同等稀疏度的随机掩码以及无约束更新三种配置，同时保持遗忘目标、优化计划和计算预算固定。

Result: 三种配置在线性探测、原型恢复和层级CKA等多项表示层面评估中表现出统计上等价的表示恢复性；遗忘梯度高度集中在网络最后几层（CIFAR-10上约92%的平方梯度能量）；显著性掩码的类别特异性有限（特异性指数0.09-0.11），不同遗忘类选择的参数子集高度重叠。

Conclusion: 在所研究场景中，表示层面的遗忘主要由梯度集中和表示几何特性主导，而非显著性选择的权重身份；有效的表示层面遗忘需要直接作用于潜在表示的目标函数，而非日益复杂的权重选择策略。

Abstract: Machine unlearning aims to remove the influence of specific training data while preserving model utility. Many state-of-the-art approaches pursue this goal by restricting the forgetting update to a subset of parameters selected through gradient-based saliency. Although such methods are widely adopted, the actual contribution of saliency-based weight selection to representation-level forgetting remains unclear. In this work, we perform the first controlled ablation of the saliency masking mechanism used by SalUn. Using a matched-compute experimental design on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 with ResNet-18, we compare saliency-based masking against random masks of equal sparsity and unconstrained updates, while keeping the unlearning objective, optimization schedule, and computational budget fixed. Across multiple representation-level evaluations, including linear probing, prototype recovery, and layer-wise CKA, the three configurations exhibit statistically equivalent representation-level recoverability. We find that forget gradients are strongly concentrated in the final network layers (approximately 92% of the squared gradient energy on CIFAR-10) before any mask is applied, causing all masking strategies to operate within the same representational subspace. Furthermore, saliency masks show limited class specificity (specificity index 0.09-0.11), selecting highly overlapping parameter subsets across different forget classes. Our findings suggest that, in the studied setting, representation-level forgetting is primarily governed by gradient concentration and representation geometry rather than by the specific identity of saliency-selected weights. More broadly, the results support a growing body of evidence indicating that effective representation-level unlearning requires objectives that act directly on latent representations rather than on increasingly sophisticated weight-selection strategies.

</details>


### [97] [Emergent Misalignment Recruits a Pre-existing Persona Subspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21356)
*Mohammed Suhail B Nadaf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究发现，对齐语言模型在狭窄的不良建议数据上微调会导致广泛的错位，原因是微调激活了模型中预先存在的“人格结构”。通过提取和操控这一低秩子空间，可以预防或诱导错位行为，揭示了模型对齐的脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者旨在理解为何在狭窄的不良数据上微调会导致模型在无关领域出现广泛的错位行为（emergent misalignment），即窄范围的训练如何泛化到广泛的不相关领域。

Method: 使用对比教师强迫技术从冻结的指令微调模型（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct）中提取领域人格子空间，发现四个不相关领域共享一个低秩核心子空间。通过投影操作将该子空间从残差流中移除或注入，以及对权重梯度进行干预，系统性地测试该结构的作用。

Result: 窄范围微调激活了模型预存的宽错位人格结构：移除该子空间可将错位率从27.7%降至0%，注入则可使错位率升至45.4%。该结构在微调第一步即被激活，且无法通过事后权重编辑完全消除。同一预算的不良数据分散在四个领域比简单叠加产生更多错位。

Conclusion: 窄范围微调导致的广泛错位源于激活模型中预存的跨领域人格结构，而非简单的权重修改。该结构具有鲁棒性，其干预虽能阻止错位但也会消除窄范围训练行为，揭示了对齐机制的深层脆弱性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning an aligned language model on a narrow stream of bad advice can make it broadly misaligned on questions unrelated to the training data, a phenomenon called emergent misalignment. We ask why the narrow lesson generalizes at all, and we find that narrow fine-tuning recruits a persona structure that is present in the model before the fine-tune exists. From a frozen instruction-tuned model (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct) we extract per-domain persona subspaces by contrastive teacher forcing and find that 4 unrelated domains share one low-rank core at 657x a random-subspace null, with 82% of that core lying outside a style core built at matched diversity. The literal first optimizer step of fine-tuning on insecure code climbs a broad-misalignment margin harder than the same code framed as educational, and forecasts realized margin movement out to 375 steps. Projecting the subspace out of the residual stream throughout fine-tuning prevents broad misalignment (27.7% to 0.0% of judged generations) while a matched-rank random subspace changes nothing; injecting it into the never-fine-tuned model induces misalignment that grows with dose to 45.4%, past the fine-tuned model it is measured against. The same projection applied to the weight gradient is inert, and three post-hoc weight edits leave the disposition in place: the sharpest edit suppresses the behavior rather than removing it, and the ablated structure re-forms inside the subspace the edit cleared. Spreading a fixed budget of bad data across 4 domains produces more broad misalignment than mechanical weight superposition and matched diversity jointly account for. All measurements come from one model at 14B; the extraction is from an aligned instruction-tuned checkpoint, which leaves the structure's provenance open; and the intervention that prevents misalignment also abolishes the narrow trained behavior.

</details>


### [98] [Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE): A Mathematical Framework for Deconstructing Learned Representations in Deep Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21366)
*Hossein Mobahi,Peter L. Bartlett*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HOPE框架，通过将网络压缩从离散域转换到连续函数的Hilbert空间，统一了剪枝和神经元合并操作，实现了无数据和无超参数的网络表示分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络编码了复杂的表示，但解构这些内部知识仍然是一个挑战。虽然学习与压缩之间存在联系，网络压缩为分析这些知识提供了有希望的视角，但标准压缩启发式方法经常受到尺度对称性和架构偏见的影响。

Method: 作者引入了Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE)数学框架，将网络压缩从离散域转移到连续函数的Hilbert空间。通过将单个神经元建模为秩1的Hilbert-Schmidt算子，HOPE将剪枝和神经元合并统一为低秩子空间投影。进一步扩展该公式，HOPE引入宏块驱逐来涵盖多层结构（如整个残差路径），使用统一的度量标准。

Result: HOPE是一个无数据和无超参数的框架，能够在不同类型和大小的层之间实现无偏的架构决策。概念验证实验在模型压缩和微调任务中展示了该理论的实践潜力。

Conclusion: HOPE框架成功地将网络压缩问题转换为Hilbert空间中的数学问题，提供了一种统一的理论基础来分析神经网络的内部表示，为网络压缩和知识解构提供了新的视角和实用工具。

Abstract: Deep neural networks encode complex representations, but deconstructing this internal knowledge remains a challenge. Given the link between learning and compression, network compression offers a promising lens to analyze this knowledge. However, standard compression heuristics often suffer from scale symmetries and architectural biases. To resolve these, we introduce Hilbert Operator for Progressive Encoding (HOPE), a mathematical framework to gradually deconstruct the representations in trained network weights.
  HOPE shifts network compression from the discrete domain into a Hilbert space of continuous functions. By modeling individual neurons as rank-1 Hilbert-Schmidt operators, HOPE unifies pruning and neuron merging as low-rank subspace projection. Extending this formulation, HOPE introduces macro block eviction to encompass multi-layer structures like entire residual pathways under the same unified metric. This unified approach enables unbiased architectural decisions across layers with different types and sizes. HOPE is a data-free and hyperparameter-free framework. We present proof-of-concept experiments in model compression and fine-tuning to highlight the practical potential of our theory.

</details>


### [99] [Mean-to-Score Discrete Diffusion: Posterior-Mean Denoisers for Score Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21372)
*Jingyuan Li,Xiaoyi Jiang,Yixuan Jiang,Wei Liu,Yi Zhu,Zuoqiang Shi,Pipi Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了M2S方法，通过预测清洁令牌后验均值并将其转换为分数，解决了SEDD中的贝叶斯可实现性问题，显著提升了生成质量和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SEDD虽然通过正性约束保证了非负的逆向跳转率，但未能确保贝叶斯可实现性：在噪声状态下的分数比率不必由前向核下的任何清洁令牌后验联合诱导。训练好的SEDD检查点中约四分之一的完整分数向量违反坐标框约束，超过一半虽然位于框内但仍与任何有效后验不兼容，这可能导致有限步采样中的负预归一化权重。

Method: 提出mean-to-score（M2S）方法：预测清洁令牌后验均值，通过精确的核相关线性映射将其转换为分数。该构造适用于任何满足温和支持条件的已知坐标连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）。对于均匀破坏，将概率单纯形映射到桥多面体；对于吸收掩码破坏，结果目标精确恢复MD4。同时引入将原始分数投影到桥多面体的方法来消除负权重。

Result: 在28.4M参数的CIFAR-10对照实验中，M2S将测试BPD从3.173降至3.129，FID-50k也有所改善。投影方法将生成PPL从203.6改善至175.1。170M参数的M2S模型在262B OpenWebText令牌槽上训练，在每个测试采样预算下均优于SEDD、GIDD和Neural CTMC基线，在128步时达到生成PPL 143.3，而最强的纯均匀基线为183.6。

Conclusion: M2S方法成功解决了SEDD中的贝叶斯可实现性问题，通过确保分数向量与有效后验兼容，消除了采样中的负权重问题，在多个基准测试中取得了显著的性能提升，证明了该方法在离散扩散模型中的有效性和优越性。

Abstract: Score Entropy Discrete Diffusion (SEDD) parameterizes discrete reverse processes with unconstrained positive score ratios. While positivity guarantees nonnegative reverse jump rates, it does not ensure Bayes realizability: ratios at a noisy state need not be jointly induced by any clean-token posterior under the forward kernel. The score-entropy loss has the correct population optimum but does not enforce this constraint away from it. In a trained pure-uniform SEDD checkpoint, roughly one quarter of complete score vectors violate the coordinate box, while more than half lie inside it yet remain materially incompatible with any valid posterior. Such violations can produce negative pre-normalization weights in finite-step sampling. Projecting raw scores onto the bridge polytope removes all observed negative weights and improves external generative PPL from $203.6$ to $175.1$ without changing the sampler. We introduce \emph{mean-to-score} (M2S), which predicts a clean-token posterior mean and converts it to the score through an exact kernel-dependent linear map. The construction applies to any known coordinate-wise continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) satisfying a mild support condition. For uniform corruption, it maps the probability simplex onto the bridge polytope; for absorbing-mask corruption, the resulting objective recovers MD4 exactly. In a controlled 28.4M-parameter CIFAR-10 comparison, M2S lowers test BPD from $3.173$ to $3.129$ and FID-50k from $\CifarSEDDFID$ to $\CifarMtwoSFID$. A 170M-parameter M2S model trained on about 262B OpenWebText token slots outperforms the evaluated pure-uniform SEDD, GIDD, and Neural CTMC checkpoints at every tested sampling budget, reaching generative PPL $143.3$ at 128 steps versus $183.6$ for the strongest pure-uniform baseline.

</details>


### [100] [A Diffusion-Model Subpopulation Digital Twin for Mobile Health Deployment: A Case Study on the HeartSteps Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21403)
*Ziping Xu,Yuyi Chang,Chenshun Ni,Nithin Sugavanam,Asim H. Gazi,Pedja Klasnja,Emre Ertin,Susan A. Murphy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了“JITAI-Twins”方法，通过条件时间序列扩散模型构建目标子人群的数字孪生，用于在即时自适应干预部署前比较候选在线算法。该方法支持三阶段信息更新：大规模观察数据预训练、相关人群干预数据微调、领域专家知识校准，并在HeartSteps系列研究中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动健康干预越来越多地使用在线学习和决策算法来个性化推送健康行为提醒，但设计不当的算法可能导致用户负担和参与度下降。因此在实际部署前，需要针对真实的模拟用户来验证新的算法设计决策，以避免在真实人群中直接测试不成熟的算法。

Method: 提出基于条件时间序列扩散模型的方法来构建“JITAI-Twins”数字孪生。该方法具有时间一致性（未来行动不影响生成的过去），并支持三阶段信息更新：1）大规模观察数据集预训练；2）相关人群的小规模先前干预部署微调；3）基于领域专家知识对目标人群进行推理时校准。

Result: 在HeartSteps系列（v2至v4）物理活动建议干预部署的每个预部署阶段进行验证，将每个后续部署视为即将开展的研究。结果表明，该方法比简单模拟器更好地重现了目标子人群的时间结构和参与者间结构。

Conclusion: 研究结果证明所提出的数字孪生可以用于在实际部署前模拟目标部署，这为测试和指导在线算法设计决策提供了前提条件，从而有助于在移动健康干预中实现更好的算法设计。

Abstract: Mobile-health interventions increasingly use online learning and decision making algorithms to personalize when to nudge users toward healthier behavior, but a poorly designed algorithm can burden and disengage participants. New algorithm design decisions should therefore be vetted against realistic simulated users before each real-life deployment. We propose a method to develop ``JITAI-Twins'': digital twins of a target subpopulation for comparing candidate online algorithms before a just-in-time adaptive intervention (JITAI) deployment. The method builds on a conditional time-series diffusion model that is temporally consistent (future actions do not affect the generated past), and it supports repeated updating from three sources of information, in three steps: pre-training on a large observational dataset, fine-tuning on small prior intervention deployments in related populations, and inference-time calibration to the next target population from domain-scientist expertise. We validate the twin at each pre-deployment stage of the long-running HeartSteps series (v2 through v4) of physical-activity suggestion intervention deployments, treating each successive deployment as an upcoming study. The proposed method reproduces the target subpopulation's temporal and between-participant structure better than simpler simulators. These results suggest that our twin can be used to simulate a target deployment before it runs, the prerequisite for testing and informing online algorithm design decisions.

</details>


### [101] [Semantic-Aware Task Clustering for Constructive and Cooperative Multi-Tasking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21426)
*Ahmad Halimi Razlighi,Maximilian H. V. Tillmann,Edgar Beck,Bho Matthiesen,Armin Dekorsy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向CMT-SemCom的语义感知任务聚类方法，通过将语义对齐的任务分组训练，有效避免了破坏性合作和负迁移，提升了任务执行精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现合作多任务语义通信中的合作效应并非总是有益的：当任务间语义关系不当时，多任务合作反而会造成破坏性合作（destructive cooperation）和负迁移（negative transfer），降低系统性能。因此需要一种方法来识别并组合语义相关的任务，确保合作的积极性。

Method: 提出了一种语义感知的任务聚类框架，将问题建模为顺序多阶段优化问题。具体分为两个阶段：（i）利用层次化密度空间聚类方法对语义对齐的任务进行聚类；（ii）在发现的任务组内进行端到端联合训练。该方法在短暂初始训练后一次性完成聚类，随后仅在组内进行E2E训练。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法有效缓解了破坏性合作与负迁移问题，相较于无聚类的多任务学习基线和单任务训练基线，均获得了精度提升。

Conclusion: 通过识别并聚类语义对齐的任务，可以显著提升CMT-SemCom系统的合作效益，为语义通信中的多任务协作提供了有效的任务分组策略。

Abstract: Cooperative multi-task semantic communication (CMT-SemCom) improves task execution performance by leveraging shared representations. However, as we demonstrated in [1], cooperative multi-tasking can be either constructive or destructive, depending on the semantic relationships among tasks. To ensure constructive cooperation, we propose a semantic-aware task clustering method for CMT-SemCom. We have formulated a sequential multi-stage optimization problem in which semantically aligned tasks are clustered once after a short initial training phase, and then end-to-end (E2E) joint training is conducted exclusively within the discovered groups. Specifically, the problem decomposes into two stages: (i) a semantic clustering problem leveraging hierarchical density-based spatial clustering, and (ii) an intra-cluster E2E CMT-SemCom learning problem. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates destructive cooperation and negative transfer, yielding accuracy gains compared to unclustered multi-tasking and individual training baselines.

</details>


### [102] [Context-weighted Discrete Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21427)
*Daniil Cherniavskii,Daniel Severo,Karen Ullrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于局部上下文加权的离散流匹配方法，通过上下文加权采样器和缩放交叉熵损失函数，在不增加计算开销的情况下将生成困惑度降低高达63%，同时保持了灵活的生成顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准离散流匹配的分解训练目标使模型面临不同难度的目标token，其中高熵、模糊的token与可预测的token混合在一起，影响模型性能。作者通过实验发现token的不确定性与其邻域上下文的密度密切相关，这启发了利用局部上下文信息改进模型的思路。

Method: 提出了两种简单而有效的改进方法：(1) 上下文加权采样器：通过修改底层连续时间马尔可夫链(CTMC)融入局部上下文信息，提高生成质量且计算开销可忽略；(2) 缩放交叉熵损失函数：根据不同token重新加权训练信号，降低生成困惑度。

Result: 在OpenWebText数据集上，生成困惑度降低高达63%；生成质量与强半自回归块扩散基线相当，同时保留了任意顺序生成的能力；采样和训练效率均显著提升。

Conclusion: 局部上下文是离散生成建模中的关键因素，简单的上下文感知改进能够显著提升采样和训练效率，为离散生成模型的优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Discrete flow matching provides a flexible framework for generative modeling on discrete structures. However, the standard factorized training objective exposes the model to targets of varying difficulty, mixing well-conditioned, predictable tokens with ambiguous, high-entropy ones. We empirically demonstrate that the uncertainty over the value of each token is closely related to the density of available context in its neighborhood. Motivated by this observation, we propose a simple modification to the underlying continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) that incorporates local context information. Our context-weighted sampler improves generation quality with negligible computational overhead, while our scaled cross-entropy loss function reweights the training signal from different tokens and reduces generative perplexity by up to 63% on OpenWebText. Moreover, our approach matches a strong semi-autoregressive block diffusion baseline in quality while retaining the ability to perform generation in any order. These results highlight the role of local context as an important factor in discrete generative modeling and show that simple context-aware modifications can significantly improve both sampling and training efficiency.

</details>


### [103] [KroQuant: Kronecker-Structured Block Transforms for Efficient Post-Training Quantization of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21446)
*Yann Bouquet,Alireza Khodamoradi,Kristof Denolf,Mathieu Salzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了KroQuant，一种针对扩散变换器(DiTs)的W4A4后训练量化方法，通过学习Kronecker结构的可逆变换解决激活值离群点问题，在保持量化精度的同时显著降低推理开销，在多个模型和数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散变换器(DiTs)的W4A4后训练量化会导致输出质量严重下降，主要原因是激活值中存在4位格式无法表示的离群点。现有方法存在权衡困境：逐通道缩放(SmoothQuant)计算开销低但损害量化精度；固定Hadamard变换量化精度好但在线开销大；学习的全维度可逆变换校准效果最佳但每层每步需要昂贵的稠密矩阵乘法。需要一种既能保持量化精度又能降低推理开销的方法。

Method: 提出KroQuant方法：1) 对激活值的每个32元素块应用学习的Kronecker结构可逆变换，参数量不到逐通道缩放的一半；2) 块局部结构以小型张量核心GEMM运行，在MI350 GPU上量化器内核比SmoothQuant快14%；3) 离线LoRaQ权重校准吸收残差权重量化误差。

Result: 在MI350 GPU上，KroQuant量化器内核比SmoothQuant内核快14%。在PixArt-Σ、SANA和FLUX.1-schnell模型上进行W4A4(MXFP4e2)量化，在MJHQ-30K和SDCI数据集上，KroQuant产生的输出比SVDQuant和LoRaQ更接近FP参考值，同时保持或改善图像质量。

Conclusion: KroQuant成功解决了扩散变换器W4A4量化中的精度与效率权衡问题，通过Kronecker结构的可逆变换实现了比现有方法更好的量化效果和更低的推理开销，为高性能扩散模型的高效部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) of diffusion transformers (DiTs) to W4A4 severely degrades output quality, because activations entering each linear layer contain outliers that 4-bit formats cannot represent. The standard fix applies an invertible linear transform to the activations and its inverse to the weights before quantizing both. Normalization layers between blocks force this transform to run online at every denoising step, making its inference computation cost the binding design constraint. Existing options trade quantization quality for inference cost: per-channel scaling (SmoothQuant) is computationally cheap but impacts the magnitude of the channels, which can harm quantization accuracy; fixed Hadamard transforms yield better quantization accuracy but require large block sizes that incur a high online cost; learned full-$d$ invertible transforms calibrate best but entail a prohibitive dense $d \times d$ matrix multiplication (GEMM) per layer per step.
  We propose KroQuant, a PTQ method that applies a learned Kronecker-structured invertible transform to each 32-element block of the activation, storing less than half the parameters of per-channel scaling. The block-local structure runs as small tensor-core GEMMs, and on an MI350 GPU the KroQuant quantizer kernel is up to $14\%$ faster than the SmoothQuant kernel. Offline LoRaQ weight calibration then absorbs the residual per-weight quantization error. On PixArt-$Σ$, SANA, and FLUX.1-schnell at W4A4 (MXFP4e2), KroQuant produces outputs closer to the FP reference than SVDQuant and LoRaQ on MJHQ-30K and SDCI, while preserving or improving image quality.

</details>


### [104] [Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21453)
*Rajiv Shailesh Chitale,Rahul Madhavan,Taneesh Gupta,Deepanway Ghosal,Aravindan Raghuveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TTEL算法，通过token级别的错误定位实现高效的推理时计算扩展，在编程和数学推理任务上以更低的计算成本获得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推理时计算扩展方法（如独立采样和顺序多轮细化）缺乏token级别的信用分配机制，导致有效推理前缀被频繁丢弃，造成计算资源浪费。需要一种能够精确识别错误位置并重用有效前缀的方法来提高计算效率。

Method: 提出Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL)算法。该算法利用固定反馈或环境反馈进行token级别的错误定位：通过比较在有信息反馈条件下的条件概率与零上下文基线，隔离出错误发生的步骤。然后截断轨迹并分支新的生成过程，最大化重用有效前缀。

Result: TTEL在顺序推理领域建立了严格占优的帕累托前沿（以pass-at-k与生成token成本衡量）。在LiveCodeBench上，Qwen3-8B使用TTEL达到71.0%的pass@64，同时生成约360.4k个token，而独立采样需要735.0k个token。在AIME-2025和HMMT-2025数学基准测试中，TTEL在Qwen3-8B和Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507上均优于竞争基线。

Conclusion: TTEL通过token级别的错误定位和有效前缀重用，显著提升了推理时计算扩展的效率，在编程和数学推理任务上实现了更好的性能-成本权衡，为大规模语言模型的推理优化提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: Scaling inference-time computation has emerged as a reliable method to improve the performance of large language models on complex reasoning and programming tasks. However, standard approaches such as independent sampling and sequential multi-turn refinement operate without token-level credit assignment, resulting in computational inefficiency, since valid reasoning prefixes are frequently discarded. In this work, we introduce Test-Time Scaling via Error Localization (TTEL), an inference-time algorithm that utilizes fixed or environment feedback to perform token-level error localization. By comparing conditional probabilities under informed feedback against a null-context baseline, TTEL isolates the step at which an error occurred. The algorithm then truncates the trajectory and branches a new generation, maximally reusing the valid prefix. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that TTEL establishes strictly dominating Pareto frontiers across sequential reasoning domains, measured by pass-at-k vs. generated-token cost. With Qwen3-8B on LiveCodeBench, TTEL attains a pass@64 of 71.0% while generating approximately half as many tokens as independent sampling (360.4k vs. 735.0k). Generalizing to math benchmarks AIME-2025 and HMMT-2025, TTEL cleanly outperforms competing test-time baselines across both Qwen3-8B and Qwen3-4B-Thinking-2507.

</details>


### [105] [Error Certificates for KV-Cache Eviction via Randomized Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21475)
*Peng Xie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了确定性KV缓存驱逐无法识别被删除内容的影响，而随机化驱逐可以通过调查采样方差估计器提供误差证书，虽然不能预测失败，但能有效归因缓存引起的错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型语言模型推理中，KV缓存驱逐是一种常用的内存优化技术。然而，确定性的驱逐策略（保留top-k重要token，删除其余）存在一个根本性问题：系统无法知道被删除的值对最终输出的影响有多大。这引发了对现有驱逐策略可靠性的质疑。

Method: 作者从理论角度证明了确定性驱逐策略存在不可识别性问题。随后提出随机化驱逐方案：使用Poisson采样以已知包含概率保留尾部token，通过logit偏移在softmax内部进行Hájek校正，并应用调查采样方差估计器来构建每步误差证书。

Result: 在真实工作负载上预注册了七个声明，验证了三个：在25-50%预算下的问答感知驱逐几乎无代价；输出对数概率比证书更能预测失败；证书门控的预算升级没有效果。关键发现是：证书能有效区分缓存引起和固有失败（AUC 0.73-0.75，对比输出置信度的0.47-0.54），在重新计算调度上优于随机或置信度门控。

Conclusion: 随机化驱逐策略虽然无法预测失败，但能够实现错误归因。误差证书可以准确区分缓存导致的失败和模型固有的失败，这对于系统的可靠性监控和错误诊断具有重要价值。研究强调了在关键应用中采用随机化方法来获得可解释性的必要性。

Abstract: Deterministic KV-cache eviction keeps the top-$k$ tokens under an importance score and deletes the rest. We prove that this design cannot know what it destroyed: evicted values can be altered so that everything the serving system retains is unchanged while the true attention-output error grows arbitrarily, so no serving-time estimator of that error is consistent. Randomized eviction restores identifiability. With a Poisson-sampled tail at known inclusion probabilities, one logit offset performs the Hájek correction inside the softmax, and a survey-sampling variance estimator over the retained set becomes a per-step error certificate with 0.97 empirical coverage at no accuracy cost. On real workloads we pre-registered seven claims and lost three: question-aware eviction at 25--50\% budgets is nearly free; output log-probability predicts failure better than the certificate; certificate-gated budget escalation adds nothing. What survives is attribution: the certificate separates cache-induced from inherent failures (AUC 0.73--0.75, against 0.47--0.54 for output confidence) and schedules recomputation better than random or confidence gating. Randomization buys attribution, not prediction.

</details>


### [106] [Finite-Sample Coverage Audits for High-Recall Candidate Generation: Certification and Learning-Theoretic Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21480)
*Martin Anthony,Kaveh Salehzadeh Nobari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何用有限的审计标签来高置信度地证明初始筛选阶段遗漏的相关内容很少，证明了必须对“排除池”进行抽样才能得到有效证明，并提供了精确的有限样本工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经验数据流水线中，初始高召回阶段决定哪些项目进入后续环节，该阶段遗漏的相关项目将对后续所有阶段产生不可逆的影响。因此，如何用有限的审计标签量来证明遗漏的相关内容质量很小，是一个关键问题。

Method: 首先证明仅使用候选集内部标签无法证明遗漏质量的有意义界限；然后通过有限语料下界证明，在零遗漏情形下，任何有效的审计程序必须检查约N₀/m个排除池标签。基于此，开发了使用二项分布和超几何分布逆变换的精确有限样本工具包，用于证明遗漏质量、转换为召回率、同时证明预定义候选生成器族，以及生成针对扰动机制的应力测试证书。

Result: 证明了排除池审计在零遗漏情形下具有极小极大率最优性；开发了能够证明遗漏质量、将其通过双池设计转换为召回率、同时对预定义候选生成器族进行证明、并生成应力测试证书的有限样本工具包。

Conclusion: 排除池审计不仅是便利的选择，更是遗漏质量证明的极小极大率最优方法。所提供的有限样本工具包能够在候选生成器或其预定义族以及审计规则在检查证明标签之前固定的情况下，有效证明遗漏质量，并结合可观察的审查负担选择满足遗漏质量目标的最小负担候选生成器。

Abstract: An initial high-recall stage in an empirical pipeline decides which items pass to later review, labelling, or modelling, and relevant items it misses are lost to every subsequent stage. We study how many audit labels are needed to certify, with finite-sample validity, that this missed relevant mass is small, and our main results characterise the label complexity of this problem. We first show that no procedure using only labels from inside the candidate set can certify any non-trivial bound on the missed mass: the audit must sample the excluded pool, the only region where unrecovered relevant items can lie. We then prove a matching finite-corpus lower bound. Any valid audit that certifies fewer than $m$ missed relevant items with high probability when none are present, even if adaptive and permitted to label the entire included pool, must inspect on the order of $N_0/m$ excluded-pool labels. Excluded-pool auditing is therefore minimax rate-optimal, not merely convenient, for missed-mass certification in the zero-miss regime. Building on this characterisation, we develop an exact finite-sample toolkit, using binomial and hypergeometric inversion rather than asymptotic approximation, that certifies missed mass, converts it to recall through a two-pool design, certifies pre-specified families of nested candidate generators simultaneously, and produces stress-test certificates against declared perturbation mechanisms. These certificates can be paired with observable review burden to select the least burdensome pre-specified candidate generator meeting a missed-mass target. Every guarantee holds under one discipline: the candidate generator, or the pre-specified family from which it is selected, and the audit rule are fixed before the certification labels are examined.

</details>


### [107] [Windowed-MTP: Removing the Full-Context Draft-KV Tax at Million-Token Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21535)
*Alagappan Valliappan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长上下文场景下MTP推测解码中草案头注意力计算成本过高的问题，提出了一种无训练的Windowed-MTP方法，通过在草案注意力中应用滑动窗口和注意力汇点，显著降低了草案的KV读取成本，同时保持了目标模型的验证质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型中内置的MTP草案头在百万token长上下文场景下面临严重性能问题：草案头需要在每个草案步骤对整个KV缓存执行完整注意力计算，导致读取成本随上下文长度线性增长，在最需要推测解码的长上下文场景中反而成为瓶颈。这种效应随着草案长度增加而加剧（深度原生草案甚至可能比无推测更慢），在混合/线性注意力目标模型中尤为突出。

Method: 提出Windowed-MTP方法，仅对草案的注意力机制应用StreamingLLM风格的滑动窗口加注意力汇点技术，同时保持验证阶段的完整注意力机制不变。该方法无需训练、可直接替换、且构造上无损——完整注意力的目标模型仍然决定每个被接受的token，窗口化仅改变提议的token而不改变被接受的token。通过紧凑的环形缓冲区回收未读取的草案KV。

Result: 在三种架构家族（Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B和Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B）上，在单GPU的SGLang中处理1M上下文时，窗口化将每个解码步骤的成本相比原生MTP草案降低了28%-44%，这一输入不变的边际随上下文长度增加而扩大。在匹配接受率的情况下端到端解码延迟改善相同幅度。草案的KV工作集被限制为常数，在1M上下文时丢弃约99%的KV条目，未读取的草案KV（1M时占总KV的7.7-11%）通过环形缓冲区回收。

Conclusion: Windowed-MTP成功解决了长上下文场景下MTP草案头的性能瓶颈问题，在保持目标模型验证输出分布的前提下，显著降低了推测解码的成本，使推测解码在长上下文场景中真正发挥价值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates autoregressive generation by having a cheap draft propose tokens that a target verifies in parallel. Frontier models increasingly ship a built-in Multi-Token-Prediction (MTP/NEXTN) draft head under the assumption that the draft is negligibly cheap. At million-token context this breaks: an MTP draft head typically runs full attention over the entire KV cache at every draft step, so its read grows linearly with context and comes to dominate the draft cost -- precisely where speculation is most valuable. The effect compounds with draft length (a deep native draft can turn net-negative, slower than no speculation) and sharpens under hybrid/linear-attention targets, where cheaper verification leaves the draft's full-attention read exposed. We apply a StreamingLLM-style sliding window plus attention sink to the draft's attention only (Windowed-MTP), leaving full-attention verification intact. It is training-free, drop-in, and lossless by construction: the full-attention target still decides every accepted token, so windowing changes only which tokens are proposed, never which are accepted. It bounds the draft's KV working set to a constant, dropping ~99% of KV entries at 1M. Across three architecture families (Qwen GDN-MoE 35B/122B and a Mamba2-hybrid NoPE 120B) at 1M context on a single GPU in SGLang, windowing cuts the per-decode-step cost over the shipping native MTP draft by +28% to +44%, an input-invariant margin that widens with context. Since per-token latency is this cost divided by acceptance length, at matched acceptance end-to-end decode latency improves by the same amount, and more where windowing also lifts acceptance, while preserving the target's verified output distribution. Finally, the unread draft KV -- 7.7-11% of total KV at 1M -- is reclaimed via a compact ring buffer at no acceptance or quality cost.

</details>


### [108] [Zero-Flow Two-Sample Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21542)
*Yakun Wang,Leyang Wang,Song Liu,Taiji Suzuki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于零流准则的统计差异度量（ZFD）进行两样本检验的新方法（ZF2ST），通过分离见证学习和假设评估过程，在保持有效统计校准的同时实现了灵活的神经网络建模，实验证明其对结构化分布变化具有强检验功效且I类误差校准良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统两样本检验方法在处理高维、复杂分布数据时面临挑战，尤其是在保持统计有效性的同时利用灵活的神经网络表示能力。现有方法难以在检测局部分布差异的同时维持良好的类型I误差控制。因此需要一种能够学习样本局部错配模式并作为分布差异证据的方法，同时实现见证学习与假设评估的分离以允许使用复杂模型。

Method: 提出零流差异（ZFD）统计量，基于零流准则度量分布差异；设计零流两样本检验（ZF2ST）流程，核心是将见证学习与假设检验评估分离；开发基于回归和功效最大化两种方法学习见证函数；使用神经网络捕捉样本在两个分布间的局部错配方向模式作为分布差异证据，并保证统计校准的有效性。

Result: 在合成数据集和图像数据集上的实验表明，ZF2ST方法在检测结构化分布变化时能够获得强的检验功效，同时保持良好校准的I类误差率。方法成功利用神经网络灵活性且不牺牲统计有效性，验证了见证学习与假设检验分离策略的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的零流两样本检验方法通过创新的统计差异度量和见证学习-假设评估分离框架，为高维复杂数据的两样本检验提供了有效解决方案，在保持良好统计校准的前提下实现了强的检验功效，拓展了灵活机器学习模型在统计检验中的应用边界。

Abstract: We propose a new approach to two-sample testing for deciding whether two sets of samples are drawn from the same distribution. The test is built on a statistical discrepancy based on the zero-flow criterion, termed zero-flow discrepancy (ZFD). We prove the validity of ZFD and propose a practical testing procedure, termed the zero-flow two-sample test (ZF2ST). The key idea is to learn how samples from the two distributions are locally misaligned and use the resulting directional pattern as evidence of distributional difference. By separating witness learning from hypothesis evaluation, ZF2ST can use flexible neural networks while maintaining valid statistical calibration. We develop both regression-based and power-maximized approaches for learning the witness. Experiments on synthetic and image datasets demonstrate that ZF2ST can achieve strong testing power for structured distributional changes while maintaining well-calibrated type-I error.

</details>


### [109] [X$^3$-OPD: Distilling Reasoning into Large Audio-Language Models via On-Policy Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21550)
*Dongjie Fu,Di Cao,Xize Cheng,Zihan Zhang,Wenxu Jia,Yifu Chen,Shengpeng Ji,Yu Zhang,Tao Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了X³-OPD框架，通过跨模态策略蒸馏将文本大模型的推理能力迁移到音频语言模型，构建了三层对称语料库，实验证明该方法显著提升了音频推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型音频语言模型在听觉感知方面取得了显著进展，但在深度逻辑推理方面仍落后于基于文本的大型语言模型，主要原因是缺乏高质量的音频推理数据。

Method: 提出X³-OPD跨模态策略蒸馏框架，在训练过程中让学生模型基于自身声学感知生成推理轨迹，教师模型使用匹配的文本输入和验证答案提供token级指导；构建三层对称语料库，包括渲染成语音的文本推理、基于复杂声学场景的音频事件推理、以及涉及副语言线索的口语对话推理。

Result: 在MMSU、MMAU、BIG Bench Audio和MMAR数据集上的实验表明，X³-OPD显著提升了音频基础推理和思维链质量，同时在域转移下很大程度上保留了模型现有能力。

Conclusion: 该方法成功将跨模态蒸馏扩展到非语言事件、韵律和对话语境的推理，超越了仅限文本可恢复内容的传统蒸馏方法，为音频语言模型的推理能力提升提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While large audio-language models have achieved remarkable progress in auditory perception, they still lag behind text-based large language models in deep logical reasoning, primarily due to the scarcity of high-quality audio reasoning data. To bridge this gap, we propose X$^3$-OPD, a cross-modal on-policy distillation framework that transfers reasoning capabilities from a powerful text teacher to an audio-language student. During training, the student generates reasoning trajectories conditioned on its own acoustic perception, while the teacher provides token-level guidance using matched textual inputs and verified answers. We further construct a three-tier symmetric corpus covering textual reasoning rendered into speech, audio-event reasoning grounded in complex acoustic scenes, and spoken-dialogue reasoning involving paralinguistic cues. This design extends cross-modal distillation beyond textually recoverable content to reasoning grounded in non-linguistic events, prosody, and conversational context. Experiments on MMSU, MMAU, BIG Bench Audio, and MMAR demonstrate that X$^3$-OPD substantially improves audio-grounded reasoning and chain-of-thought quality while largely preserving the model's existing capabilities under domain shift.

</details>


### [110] [Graph Learning on Ensembles of Cyclic Peptides: An Investigation of Molecular Ensemble Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21561)
*Aaron Feller,Kris Deibler,Maxim Secor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EnsembleEGNN模型，通过编码构象集合而非单一构象来改进分子性质预测，预训练后显著提升环肽性质预测性能（R²从0.005提升至0.477）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子性质预测方法通常使用单一代表性构象，而许多分子在溶液中实际上以构象集合形式存在。这种简化处理忽略了分子的构象多样性，限制了预测准确性。

Method: 提出EnsembleEGNN分子集合基础模型：1）使用共享的等变图神经网络(EGNN)层编码每个构象；2）通过集合注意力块池化构象表示得到集合嵌入；3）在CREMP环肽集合数据集上采用多任务自监督预训练目标（掩码token恢复、噪声坐标重建、成对距离重建）；4）可与BERT序列编码器端到端协同训练。

Result: 在CREMP-CycPeptMPDB数据集上：从零训练完全失败(R²=0.005)；预训练模型达到R²=0.477，Pearson r=0.699，超越仅序列BERT基线(R²=0.439，r=0.667)；混合模型进一步提升至R²=0.538，Pearson r=0.737。

Conclusion: 将构象集合编码为单一的热力学信息嵌入能有效改进环肽性质预测。构象集合编码方法相比单一构象方法具有显著优势，预训练对模型性能至关重要。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction from structure often uses a single representative conformation, even though many molecules exist as conformational ensembles in solution. We introduce EnsembleEGNN, a molecular ensemble foundation model that encodes an ensemble by first encoding each conformer with shared Equivariant Graph Neural Network (EGNN) layers, then pooling the resulting conformer representations with a Set Attention Block. We pretrain the model on CREMP, a cyclic peptide ensemble dataset, using a multi-task self-supervised objective combining masked token recovery, noisy-coordinate reconstruction, and pairwise distance reconstruction. On the CREMP-CycPeptMPDB dataset, training EnsembleEGNN from scratch fails entirely ($R^2=0.005$). However, the pretrained model reaches $R^2=0.477$ and Pearson $r=0.699$, outperforming the sequence-only BERT baseline ($R^2=0.439$, Pearson $r=0.667$). When EnsembleEGNN is co-trained end-to-end with the BERT sequence encoder, the hybrid model improves further to $R^2=0.538$ and Pearson $r=0.737$. These results demonstrate that encoding conformational ensembles into a single thermodynamically informed embedding improves cyclic-peptide property prediction.

</details>


### [111] [Beyond Sufficiency: Time Series Explanation with Counterfactual Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21573)
*Hongnan Ma,Yiwei Shi,Mengyue Yang,Weiru Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TimePNS框架，通过引入必要性感知机制来改进时间序列分类器的解释，解决了现有充分性导向方法可能将高重要性分配给虚假子序列的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的充分性导向时间序列解释方法可能将高重要性分配给虚假子序列，这些子序列虽然支持预测但对模型决策并非必要，因此需要一种既能识别充分又能识别必要子序列的框架。

Method: 提出TimePNS框架，采用两阶段设计：第一阶段学习可识别的因果生成过程和充分性导向的解释掩码；第二阶段基于Pearl的反事实必要性概念，对时间因素进行反事实干预，获取必要性信号来监督时间门控机制，从而通过抑制非必要成分并强调反事实必要成分来优化初始解释。

Result: 在合成和真实世界时间序列基准数据集上的实验表明，TimePNS能更准确地识别决策关键子序列，并在充分性-必要性权衡方面持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: TimePNS通过整合必要性和充分性约束，成功识别出既充分又必要的决策关键子序列，为时间序列分类器提供了更忠实的解释，有效改善了充分性-必要性权衡。

Abstract: Faithful explanations of time-series classifiers should identify subsequences that are not only sufficient to preserve a black-box model's prediction, but also necessary for maintaining it. However, existing sufficiency-oriented methods can assign high importance to spurious subsequences that support the prediction without being essential to the model's decision. We introduce \textbf{TimePNS}, a necessity-aware framework for time-series explanation. Inspired by Pearl's counterfactual notion of necessity, TimePNS assesses whether a temporal factor is necessary by intervening on it and measuring whether the original prediction is disrupted. The framework adopts a two-stage design. Stage I learns an identifiable causal generative process together with a sufficiency-oriented explanation mask. Stage II performs counterfactual interventions on temporal factors to derive necessity signals, which supervise a temporal gate that refines the initial explanation by suppressing non-essential components and emphasizing counterfactually necessary ones. Experiments on synthetic and real-world time-series benchmarks show that TimePNS more accurately identifies decision-critical subsequences and consistently improves sufficiency-necessity trade-offs over strong baselines.

</details>


### [112] [Expanding Flow Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21585)
*Sophia Tang,Pranam Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了EFlows和EFMs框架，通过引入可学习的扩展操作，实现了在连续和离散空间中生成可变维度/长度的样本，突破了传统流模型固定维度的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于流的生成模型局限于固定维度或固定序列长度，无法处理输出规模本身需要学习的问题，例如可变大小的图生成和变长序列生成。

Method: 构建了一个扩展插值路径，通过扩展算子向状态空间添加条件噪声或新token，再通过传输算子沿插值路径推演状态，两个算子组合形成联合扩展与去噪的映射，将传统固定画布流模型作为特例（扩展算子为恒等映射）包含在内。

Result: 在连续模态下实现了可变维度生成，在离散单纯形上实现了变长序列和变大小图的生成，建立了处理可变输出规模问题的统一原理框架。

Conclusion: EFlows与EFMs为输出规模可控且可学习的生成任务提供了系统化解决方案，拓展了流生成模型的适用范围。

Abstract: Flow-based generative models have enabled remarkable progress in fast and controllable generation across continuous and discrete state spaces, yet existing parameterizations are constrained to fixed dimensions or fixed sequence lengths. Here, we introduce Expanding Generative Flows (EFlows), which define flows between distributions of increasing dimensionality along an expanding interpolant that grows the state by augmenting it with conditional noise. Building on this construction, we propose Expanding Flow Maps (EFMs), a new class of flow maps that distill the expanding interpolant into efficient few-step generative models. Each EFM factors the map between any two timesteps into two learnable operations: an expand operator, which augments the state space with new coordinates or tokens conditioned on the current state, and a transport map, which pushes the expanded state forward along the interpolant. Composing these operators yields a single map that jointly expands and denoises the state, recovering existing fixed-canvas flows and flow maps as the special case in which the expand operator is the identity. We further extend the framework to the discrete simplex, enabling variable-size graph generation and variable-length sequence generation. Across both continuous and discrete modalities, we establish EFlows and EFMs as a principled framework for settings in which output size is itself a learned, controllable degree of freedom.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [113] [PortLBM: A Portable Lattice Boltzmann Tool Leveraging SYCL on AMD, NVIDIA, and Intel GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20650)
*Alexander Strack,Marcel Graf,Alexander Van Craen,Dirk Pflüger*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了基于SYCL的可移植格子玻尔兹曼方法框架PortLBM，在NVIDIA、AMD和Intel GPU上评估了不同数据布局和算法变体的性能，发现需要针对特定硬件进行配置优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着摩尔定律停滞，高性能计算转向GPU加速器，需要能够在不同硬件平台上保证移植性和效率的编程模型，因此开发了可移植的LBM框架。

Method: 构建了基于SYCL的PortLBM框架，支持跨平台GPU和实时可视化；研究了三种数据布局（stream、bundle、collision）和四种算法变体在NVIDIA、AMD、Intel GPU上的性能表现。

Result: 没有单一配置能在所有GPU厂商平台上达到最优性能；stream布局在现代NVIDIA和Intel GPU上表现最佳，bundle布局在AMD GPU上更优；双格子方案吞吐量更高，单格子方案适合内存受限场景。

Conclusion: 在日益异构的计算环境中，需要可适应、可移植的LBM软件，并针对特定系统进行调优以获得最佳性能。

Abstract: The lattice Boltzmann method (LBM) is a well-established approach for simulating fluid flows at the mesoscopic scale. With the stagnation of Moore's law, high-performance computing has shifted toward GPU accelerators, necessitating programming models that ensure both portability and efficiency across diverse hardware platforms.
  We present PortLBM, an extensible portable LBM framework built on SYCL that integrates cross-platform GPU support with interactive real-time visualization. PortLBM supports diverse simulation scenarios ranging from Kármán vortex streets and wing flows to porous media, and is designed for easy extension with new algorithms and backends. As part of a performance portability study, we evaluate PortLBM on contemporary GPU architectures from NVIDIA, AMD, and Intel, examining the impact of three data layouts (stream, bundle, and collision) and four algorithmic variants on simulation throughput.
  Our results show that no single configuration achieves optimal performance across all GPU vendors, confirming the need for system-specific tuning. The stream layout maximizes bandwidth and performs best on the contemporary NVIDIA and Intel GPUs, while the bundle layout improves cache efficiency and excels on the AMD GPU. Two-lattice schemes achieve higher throughput while one-lattice schemes are preferable under memory constraints. Our work underscores the necessity for adaptable, portable LBM software in increasingly heterogeneous computing environments.

</details>


### [114] [A Framework for Reputation Aware Uninorm-driven Consensus Algorithms for Blockchain Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20700)
*Bruno Ramos-Cruz,Javier Andreu-Perez,David Richerby,Luis Martínez*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于直觉模糊集(IFS)和Uninorm聚合操作(UAO)的新型声誉共识算法框架，通过模糊化声誉表达和动态聚合机制，解决了传统共识算法中存在的算力/权益集中化问题，实现了更公平包容的区块链共识机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链共识算法存在两个主要问题：(1)工作量证明等机制需要巨大算力，导致能源浪费；(2)权益证明等机制需要高额质押，导致权力集中和参与者排斥。这些缺陷违背了区块链去中心化的核心理念，亟需设计更公平、包容的共识机制。

Method: 采用直觉模糊集(IFS)量化验证者声誉，有效表达声誉值固有的不确定性；利用Uninorm聚合操作(UAO)对声誉进行时序监控和聚合，平衡正面与负面声誉的影响。该框架在底层共识协议基础上构建，保持线性计算复杂度且不引入额外通信开销。

Result: 实验验证表明该方法在性能和评估指标上均有提升；算法保持线性时间复杂度；无额外通信开销；能有效追踪声誉变化并允许验证者在后续过程中弥补过往失误。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功实现了更公平包容的共识算法设计，在保持计算效率和通信轻量的前提下，为区块链网络公平性和参与度提供了有效解决方案，对推动区块链技术健康发展具有重要意义。

Abstract: The operation of blockchain is governed by consensus algorithms (CA). Several consensus mechanisms require significant computational power, while others necessitate high amounts of stakes to select the participant to validate and verify the transactions in the block, leading to centralisation of power and participant exclusion. This paper proposes a novel methodology to address these issues in reputation-based consensus algorithms by studying the reputation behaviour of the validator using intuitionistic fuzzy sets (IFSs) and uninorm aggregation operations (UAOs). Our approach uses IFSs to express the "reputation" because the reputation values in a consensus algorithm eventually imply uncertainty, and IFSs facilitate the representation of a lack of precise knowledge about reputation. Moreover, this methodology utilises uninorm aggregation operations to monitor reputation over time and reinforces the importance of negative and positive reputation. Consequently, this solution allows validators to rectify past failures in subsequent verification processes and foster an equitable consensus algorithm design. The proposed framework maintains linear computational complexity and does not introduce additional communication overhead beyond the underlying consensus protocol. Supported by experimental results, our methodology demonstrates improved performance and evaluation, promising advancements in blockchain network fairness and inclusivity.

</details>


### [115] [The Consensus Number of Untraceable Cryptocurrencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20929)
*Christian Cachin,David Lehnherr,Juan Villacis,François-Xavier Wicht*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文对比分析了两种加密货币发送者不可追踪方案(LUAT和CUAT)的共识特性:LUAT共识数为2,无饥饿现象但存储开销大;CUAT共识数随掩盖集大小二次增长,存在饥饿问题但存储恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发送者不可追踪性通过将交易账户隐藏在掩盖集中来实现隐私保护,现有两种设计范式:经典方案保留整个掩盖集并添加nullifier标记已花费账户,导致账本不断增长;恒定状态方案则消耗并替换整个掩盖集。本文旨在研究这两种设计对同步机制的具体影响。

Method: 将两种设计形式化为线性不可追踪资产转移对象(LUAT)和恒定不可追踪资产转移对象(CUAT),并将其定位到共识层次结构中。引入掩盖集冲突图来建模CUAT中交易的互斥关系,分析弱不可追踪性和强不可追踪性两种隐私保护模型下的共识特性。

Result: LUAT的共识数为2(标准资产转移为1),与掩盖集大小和不可追踪性概念无关,且无饥饿现象;通过固定掩盖集分区可实现垃圾回收而不增加共识数。CUAT中,弱不可追踪性下共识数无界,强不可追踪性下共识数恰好确定且随掩盖集大小二次增长,但存在饥饿问题。

Conclusion: 两种对象为相同隐私保护付出了不同代价:LUAT牺牲存储空间,CUAT牺牲同步效率和公平性。这为加密货币系统设计中的隐私-效率权衡提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Sender untraceability hides the account spent by a cryptocurrency transfer among a set of candidates, its masking set. What a transfer does to that set separates two designs: classical schemes retain the whole set and append a nullifier marking the spent account, so the ledger grows with every transfer; constant-state schemes instead consume and replace the entire set. We ask how this choice affects synchronization.
  We formalize the two designs as the linear and constant untraceable asset transfer objects (LUAT and CUAT) and locate them in the consensus hierarchy. In LUAT, transfers from distinct accounts commute. Its consensus number is 2, compared with 1 for standard asset transfer, independently of the masking-set size and of the untraceability notion, and LUAT is starvation-free. Partitioning the accounts into fixed masking sets lets exhausted sets be garbage-collected without increasing that number.
  In CUAT, a transfer consumes and replaces every account of its masking set, so two transfers whose sets intersect cannot both take effect. We formalize this with the conflict graph on masking sets, whose edges join sets sharing an account. Under weak untraceability, which protects a transaction in isolation, the consensus number is unbounded already for one-round protocols. Under strong untraceability, which protects against an observer of the complete history, untraceability holds on a history exactly when any two accounts sharing a masking set occur in the same number of the masking sets in it. This uniform incidence bounds the conflict graph, and matching constructions attain it, so the consensus number is determined exactly and grows quadratically in the masking-set size. Finally, CUAT is not starvation-free. The two objects therefore pay for the same privacy differently: LUAT in storage, CUAT in synchronization and fairness.

</details>


### [116] [Multimmit: Extending Blocks for Faster Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21021)
*Andrew Lewis-Pye,Patrick O'Grady*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Multimmit是一种针对n≥5f+1处理器的SMR协议，通过多链数据传播和一轮投票的共识层，实现了优雅的故障降级和快速区块排序，同时保持低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有并行区块链SMR协议在区块排序时面临权衡：认证方法延迟高，非认证方法在数据获取时性能下降，而最先进的Raptr协议仍然对顺序敏感，单个早期批次的故障就能阻碍系统优化路径。需要一种既能快速排序又能优雅处理故障的新方案。

Method: Multimmit结合了需要每视图一轮投票的共识层与多链数据传播机制。投票相对于领导者的提案进行，报告每个链的支持程度，并可证明提案之外的新区块。投票者按链报告能支持的距离，不阻塞不获取。

Result: 在时间t传播的交易区块期望在t+3δ内完成排序，最佳情况为t+2δ。故障降级优雅：故障生产者仅延迟自己链的区块，其他链至多等待一个视图。诚实链的广泛传播区块不会被领导者同时排除和定稿其领导者区块。共识流量每视图仅数十KB，独立于交易量。

Conclusion: Multimmit在保持低通信开销的同时，实现了快速区块排序和优雅的故障处理，解决了现有协议在延迟、性能降级和顺序敏感性方面的权衡问题，为高吞吐量区块链系统提供了一种更优的SMR解决方案。

Abstract: To meet the throughput demands of modern blockchain systems, protocols for State Machine Replication (SMR) increasingly have many processors disseminate blocks of transactions in parallel, with consensus then establishing a total ordering on the blocks of all producers. Such designs face a choice as to when a block may enter the ordering. Certified approaches wait for a quorum to attest a block's availability, which is robust but adds message delays to every transaction. Uncertified approaches let proposals reference blocks immediately, which is fast but degrades rapidly when referenced data must be fetched on the critical path. Raptr, the state of the art, takes a middle course, finalising the longest prefix of the leader's proposal that a quorum holds, so that no processor ever blocks or fetches. The remaining weakness is sensitivity to order: if the data behind a single early batch is withheld, the proposal finalises little or nothing, so individual faulty producers can still deny the system its optimistic path.
  We present Multimmit, a protocol for $n \ge 5f+1$ processors combining a consensus layer requiring one round of voting per view with multi-chain data dissemination. Votes are cast relative to the leader's proposal, reporting per chain how far the voter can support it, and may themselves attest fresh blocks beyond it. A transaction block disseminated at time $t$ is ordered by $t+3δ$ in expectation and $t+2δ$ at best, measured from the block's dissemination rather than the leader's proposal. Degradation under faults is graceful: a faulty producer delays only its own chain's blocks, costing other chains at most a one-view wait for placement. No leader can both finalise its leader block and exclude a fresh, well-circulated block of an honest chain. Consensus traffic is tens of kilobytes per view, independent of transaction volume.

</details>


### [117] [Solving Large Traveling Salesman Problems (TSPs) by a Recursive Clustering Algorithm and a Scalable FPGA-Based Implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21182)
*Junqi Huang,Hawraa Abbas Almurieb,T. Nandha Kumar,Haider A. F. Almurib,Fabrizio Lombardi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于递归聚类的TSP求解方法，通过FPGA硬件实现，具有良好的可扩展性和更高的运行频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旅行商问题(TSP)在各种应用中具有关键作用，持续吸引着重要的研究兴趣。需要开发一种能够处理大规模城市数量的有效路由解决方案。

Method: 采用递归聚类方法将城市划分为有限数量的簇，每个簇最多包含5个城市和自己的中心点。在簇内使用约束TSP和模拟退火技术进行路由，使用相邻簇的中心点作为路由过程的进出点。簇中心点之间的连接使用模拟退火建立。采用基于FPGA的硬件实现，利用内存存储簇信息。在距离计算方面，选择平方欧几里得距离因其资源利用率低。

Result: 该方法能够路由大量城市，由于每个簇中城市数量减少，能够提供准确且成本效益高的路由解决方案。该方法独立于FPGA的逻辑硬件，其可扩展性取决于FPGA的内存容量。与以前的方法相比，可以处理更多的城市进行路由，提供更好的可扩展性和更高的运行频率。

Conclusion: 提出的递归聚类方法结合FPGA硬件实现为大规模TSP问题提供了一种可行的解决方案，在可扩展性、处理能力和运行效率方面优于现有方法，为实际应用中的大规模路由问题提供了有效的技术途径。

Abstract: The Traveling Salesman Problem (TSP) continues to attract significant research interest due to its critical role in various applications. This paper introduces a recursive clustering approach that divides cities into a limited number of clusters, each containing up to five cities and its own centroid. Constrained TSP and simulated annealing techniques are employed to route cities within each cluster, using the centroids of neighboring clusters as entry and exit points for the routing process. This method offers the benefit of producing accurate and cost-effective routing solutions, due to the reduced number of cities in each cluster. The connections between cluster centroids are established using simulated annealing. The FPGA-based proposed hardware implementation demonstrates the feasibility of routing a large number of cities, as the approach leverages memory to store cluster information. Consequently, the method is independent of the FPGA's logic hardware, and its scalability depends on the FPGA's memory capacity. Furthermore, distance calculations using approximate methods for the FPGA execution are compared and the squared Euclidean is chosen due to its low resources' utilization. Compared to previous methods, the proposed approach can handle more cities for routing, offering better scalability and a higher operating frequency.

</details>


### [118] [Controlled Periodic Synchronization for Efficient Data-Parallel Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21224)
*Imane Ettifouri,Mostapha Zbakh,Claude Tadonki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为CPDP的分布式训练策略，通过调整同步频率来平衡通信受限环境下的准确性和训练时间。在WAN环境下，CPDP在ResNet-50/CIFAR-100上比标准DDP提高了2.44个百分点的准确率，同时减少了13.8%的训练时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据并行训练在地理分布式环境中面临通信延迟和网络变化的挑战。标准DDP每次迭代都同步梯度，对通信敏感；而周期性方法如LocalSGD主要依赖参数平均，当工作节点轨迹偏离时可能不足。本文将同步频率作为系统参数进行研究，以解决通信受限分布式训练的问题。

Method: 提出Controlled Periodic Data Parallelism (CPDP)策略，这是一种与PyTorch-DDP兼容的方法。CPDP交替执行本地更新和协调步骤，协调步骤结合了梯度AllReduce和SlowMo参数平均。实验在Grid'5000平台上进行，包括站内集群和跨站WAN部署（Nancy到Sophia，RTT为16.6ms）。

Result: 在ResNet-50/CIFAR-100固定学习率0.1设置下，CPDP在所有评估配置中获得最高峰值测试准确率。K=2时，CPDP比DDP提高2.28个百分点但增加运行时间；K=4时，CPDP比DDP提高2.44个百分点，同时平均运行时间减少13.8%。性能分析显示K=4时的同步时间约为DDP的一半。在ViT-S/CIFAR-100和ResNet-50/TinyImageNet上，CPDP与DDP竞争力强且普遍优于LocalSGD。

Conclusion: 同步频率是通信约束下分布式训练的实用控制参数。CPDP通过结合梯度AllReduce和参数平均的协调步骤，有效平衡了WAN环境下的准确性和时间权衡，为地理分布式训练提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Data-parallel training relies on frequent gradient synchronization across workers. Standard DDP synchronizes gradients at every iteration, which is effective on fast local-area networks but increasingly sensitive to communication latency and network variability in geographically distributed environments. Periodic methods such as LocalSGD reduce synchronization frequency but rely mainly on parameter averaging, which may be insufficient when worker trajectories diverge. This paper studies synchronization frequency as a systems parameter for communication-constrained distributed training. We evaluate Controlled Periodic Data Parallelism (CPDP), a PyTorch-DDP-compatible strategy that alternates local updates with a reconciliation step combining gradient AllReduce and SlowMo parameter averaging. Experiments are conducted on Grid'5000 across intra-site clusters and a cross-site WAN deployment spanning Nancy and Sophia with 16.6 ms RTT. In the main fixed learning-rate setting of 0.1 on ResNet-50/CIFAR-100, CPDP achieves the highest peak test accuracy among the evaluated configurations. At K=2, CPDP improves over DDP by 2.28 percentage points but incurs additional wall-clock time. At K=4, CPDP improves over DDP by 2.44 percentage points while reducing average wall-clock time by 13.8%. Direct profiling shows that exposed synchronization time at K=4 is roughly half that of DDP, explaining the improved WAN accuracy-time trade-off. Additional experiments on ViT-S/CIFAR-100 and ResNet-50/TinyImageNet show that CPDP remains competitive with DDP and generally improves over LocalSGD. Overall, the results show that synchronization frequency is a practical control parameter for distributed training under communication constraints.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Hardware-Software Co-Design for Float16 On-Device Training on RISC-V Single-Core](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21130)
*Benjamin Hubinet,Pierre-Alain Moellic,Olivier Savry,Olivier Potin,Jean-Baptiste Rigaud*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于RISC-V标准扩展(Zfh和Zvfh)的开源框架，实现了在资源受限的单核RISC-V设备上的完整端侧训练，相比float32减少约50%内存占用且模型性能损失极小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着边缘AI应用的普及，在资源受限的嵌入式设备上实现完整的神经网络训练能力成为重要需求。传统float32训练方法内存占用大，难以在资源受限的单核RISC-V处理器上实现，需要一种更高效的解决方案。

Method: 利用RISC-V标准扩展Zfh(标量float16)和Zvfh(向量float16)构建开源框架，基于AIfES框架进行扩展，支持自定义硬件特定函数。实现float16精度训练以减少内存占用，同时引入层冻结功能支持迁移学习和微调场景。在RV64GC超标量乱序FPGA软核上验证Zfh扩展的面积开销，并讨论Zvfh实现架构。

Result: 相比float32方案，内存占用减少约50%，模型性能损失极小。Zfh扩展在RV64GC超核上的面积开销很低：在175MHz频率下仅增加1.15% LUT6和0.05% FF。成功实现了完整的端侧训练能力并支持迁移学习场景。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了利用RISC-V标准float16扩展在资源受限单核处理器上实现高效端侧训练的可行性，具有低内存占用和低硬件开销的优势，为嵌入式AI应用提供了实用的开源解决方案。

Abstract: By leveraging standard RISC-V extensions, namely Zfh (scalar float16) and Zvfh (vector float16), this work proposes an open-source framework to enable complete on-device training on resource-constrained RISC-V single-core. Our approach allows memory footprint reduction by about 50% as compared to using float32 and with minimal model performance degradation. We also facilitate transfer learning and fine-tuning scenarios by incorporating layer-freezing capabilities. Our work builds onto AIfES, an open-source, modular and generic DNN training and inference framework for embedded systems that can be extended with custom hardware-specific functions. The benefits of float16 is further emphasized by outlining the low area overhead of Zfh on a RV64GC super-scalar out-of-order FPGA softcore (+1.15% LUT6 and +0.05% FF at 175MHz). Finally, we discuss the architecture of a Zvfh implementation within the same RISC-V core.

</details>


### [120] [Chip Floorplanning Combining Convex and Non-convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21408)
*Yilihamujiang Yimamu,Guillem Pastor-Rué,Jordi Cortadella*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合非凸和凸优化的多阶段固定轮廓布图规划框架，在MCNC、GSRC和HB+基准测试上实现了平均HPWL比现有最佳方法至少减少1%和5%的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布图规划是VLSI物理设计的关键早期阶段，其质量直接影响互连线长、芯片性能和后续设计效率。现有方法在优化线长方面仍有改进空间，需要更有效的优化框架来提升布图规划质量。

Method: 提出三阶段框架：(1)初始布图规划阶段使用二次布局优化模块连接性，生成具有强网络聚类特性的拓扑感知起始配置；(2)全局布图规划阶段采用基于Adam的投影梯度法求解非凸优化问题，最小化线长和重叠；(3)合法化阶段利用基于水平/垂直约束图的log变换模型，在凸优化框架下消除残余重叠。

Result: 在MCNC、GSRC和HB+基准测试上的实验表明，该框架达到了最先进的线长质量，与现有最佳布图规划器相比，平均HPWL分别减少至少1%和5%。

Conclusion: 通过结合非凸和凸优化方法，所提出的多阶段布图规划框架能够有效优化模块连接性并消除重叠，在保持固定轮廓约束的同时实现了线长的显著改进，为VLSI物理设计提供了更高质量的早期布图解决方案。

Abstract: Floorplanning is a critical early stage of VLSI physical design, and its quality directly impacts interconnect wirelength, chip performance, and downstream design efficiency. This article presents a multi-stage fixed-outline floorplanning framework that combines non-convex and convex optimization. The framework operates in three successive phases. In the initial floorplanning phase, a quadratic placement optimizes module connectivity to produce a topology-aware starting configuration with strong net clustering, albeit with significant module overlaps. In the global floorplanning phase, Adam-based projected gradient method is employed to solve non-convex optimization that minimizes wirelength and overlap. In the legalization phase, a log-transformed model exploiting horizontal and vertical constraint graphs eliminates residual overlaps under a convex formulation. Experiments on the MCNC, GSRC and HB+ benchmarks demonstrate that the framework achieves state-of-the-art wirelength quality, reducing average HPWL by at least 1\% and 5\% compared to state-of-the-art floorplanners on tested benchmarks.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

### [121] [pAI-Econ-claude: A Gated Human-in-the-Loop Multi-Agent Architecture for AI-Assisted Economic Theory Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21268)
*Chen Zhu,Xiaolu Wang,Weilong Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文提出了pAI-Econ-claude，一种带门控的人机协作多智能体架构，用于AI辅助的经济理论发展。在5个匹配的经济理论任务评估中，门控架构在4个任务上优于无门控基线，平均失败严重程度从1.58降至1.16，整体有用性从2.60提升至3.10。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在社会科学研究领域（如经济学），基于LLM的智能体必须输出结果，但这些结果缺乏廉价、任务完备且机器可读的正确性信号。这为多智能体系统创造了独特的可靠性问题：当没有任何组件能够认证最终结果时，应如何组织生成、批判、协调和人类判断？

Method: 提出pAI-Econ-claude架构，包含三个核心组件：（1）智能体通过共享工作空间协调，该空间包含可检查的中间记录；（2）专用门控诊断特定的失败模式并建议循环回退，但不认证正确性；（3）人类检查点保留对高逆转成本决策的权威。在5个匹配的经济理论任务上与无门控基线进行对比评估。

Result: 两名对配置盲评的评估者在所有5个成对排名上达成一致，其中4个任务偏好门控架构，1个任务偏好基线。平均失败严重程度从1.58降至1.16，整体有用性从2.60提升至3.10。最大增益出现在现实检查拒绝错误的市场结构前提以及证明审查促使修正错误的福利主张时。负面案例表明脚手架可能过度压缩经济上重要的机制。

Conclusion: 门控监督提高了AI辅助经济理论的可审计性，但不能替代形式化验证。不可逆人类判断的分配比纯智能体自主性是更有信息价值的设计变量。研究支持一个有边界的声明，为AI辅助社会科学研究提供了新的架构范式。

Abstract: In many social-science research tasks, such as economics, LLM-based agents must produce outputs for which no cheap, task-complete, machine-readable correctness signal exists. This creates a distinctive reliability problem for multi-agent systems: how should generation, critique, coordination, and human judgment be organized when no component can certify the final result? We address this problem through pAI-Econ-claude, a gated, human-in-the-loop multi-agent architecture for AI-assisted economic theory development. Agents coordinate through a shared workspace of inspectable intermediate records; specialized gates diagnose targeted failure modes and recommend loopbacks without certifying correctness; and human checkpoints retain authority over decisions that are costly to reverse. We evaluate the architecture on five matched economic-theory tasks against an ungated baseline. Two evaluators blinded to configuration agreed on all five pairwise rankings, preferring the gated architecture in four tasks and the baseline in one. Mean failure severity fell from 1.58 to 1.16, while overall usefulness rose from 2.60 to 3.10. The largest observed gain occurred when a reality check rejected a false market-structure premise and a proof review prompted revision of a false welfare claim. The negative case shows that scaffolding can also compress an economically important mechanism too aggressively. The results support a bounded claim: gated oversight improves the auditability of AI-assisted economic theory without substituting for formal verification, and the allocation of irreversible human judgment is a more informative design variable than pure agent autonomy. The workflow is publicly available at https://github.com/maxwell2732/pAI-Econ-claude.

</details>


### [122] [FedAgentKE: Federated Semantic Knowledge Evolution for Heterogeneous Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21361)
*Weihao Li,Jun Bai,Ziyang Song*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了FedAgentKE框架，实现跨异构智能体的联邦语义知识演化，通过迭代式的语义知识蒸馏、聚合和适应，在不共享原始推理轨迹的情况下实现分布式智能体框架间的协作知识转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能体框架主要孤立运行，虽然基于记忆的智能体系统通过局部检索和工作流复用改善了单个智能体，但局部经验在孤立的智能体框架间仍然碎片化，限制了跨框架知识转移和协作推理演化。

Method: 提出FedAgentKE轻量级框架，实现跨异构智能体的联邦语义知识演化。该框架使分布式智能体框架能够通过迭代式的语义知识蒸馏、聚合和适应来协作演化可转移的推理抽象，且无需共享原始推理轨迹。

Result: 在跨框架和跨任务两种设置下，实验均证明了性能的持续提升。

Conclusion: 联邦语义知识演化对未来协作智能体生态系统具有重要潜力，为解决跨框架知识转移问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents increasingly rely on reasoning, tool use, and iterative execution, yet existing agent frameworks still operate largely in isolation. While recent memory-based agent systems improve individual agents through local retrieval and workflow reuse, local experiences remain fragmented across isolated agent frameworks, limiting cross-framework knowledge transfer and collaborative reasoning evolution. We propose FedAgentKE, a lightweight framework for Federated Semantic Knowledge Evolution across heterogeneous agents. FedAgentKE enables distributed agent frameworks to collaboratively evolve transferable reasoning abstractions through iterative semantic knowledge distillation, aggregation, and adaptation without sharing raw reasoning trajectories. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements under both cross-framework and cross-task settings, highlighting the potential of federated semantic knowledge evolution for future collaborative agent ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [AINTMA: Agentic AI Architecture for Autonomous Test Management with Generative Intelligence, Secure Cloud Communication and Adaptive Quality Analytics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20452)
*Vinil Pasupuleti,Shyalendar Reddy Allala,Siva Rama Krishna Varma Bayyavarapu,Shrey Tyagi,Srinivasateja Songa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AINTMA，一个基于多智能体AI的自动化测试管理架构，通过六个专业化AI代理的协同工作，结合生成式智能和安全云通信，显著提升了软件测试效率和质量，在18个月的评估中实现了88.4%的测试优先级准确率、43%的测试周期缩减和340%的投资回报率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代软件质量保证需要能够在分布式云环境中进行自适应决策的智能、自主系统。传统的测试管理方法已无法满足云规模企业环境下的质量智能需求，亟需一种能够自主协调、智能决策的新型测试管理架构来提升测试效率和质量。

Method: AINTMA采用六层专业化AI代理架构：(1)测试发现代理、(2)风险评估代理、(3)强化学习优先级代理（将测试选择建模为马尔可夫决策过程，基于47个特征和36个月滚动窗口的历史数据学习上下文策略）、(4)执行编排代理、(5)生成式质量智能代理（使用大语言模型生成自然语言质量报告和缺陷风险摘要）、(6)云安全监控代理。代理间通过云原生微服务架构和安全多代理通信框架协调，采用零信任API网关、OAuth2/JWT认证和加密消息传递确保安全性。

Result: 在12个异构软件项目上18个月的评估表明：(1)测试优先级准确率达88.4% APFD（随机基线51.2%，最佳商业基线82.1%）；(2)测试周期时间减少43%；(3)缺陷逃逸率从8.3%降至2.1%；(4)9个月回本期实现340% ROI；(5)架构可扩展至50,000+测试用例，响应时间低于400ms；(6)生成式智能模块获得4.3/5.0的开发者实用性评分。

Conclusion: AINTMA证明了结合自主多代理协调、生成式智能和安全智能连接的代理型AI架构，能够从根本上推进云规模企业环境中的软件质量管理转型，为现代软件质量保证提供了可行的智能自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Modern software quality assurance demands intelligent, autonomous systems capable of adaptive decision-making across distributed cloud environments. This paper presents AINTMA (Agentic Intelligent Test Management Architecture), a multi-agent agentic AI system that transforms traditional test management into an autonomous quality intelligence ecosystem. AINTMA deploys six specialized AI agents (Test Discovery, Risk Assessment, Reinforcement Learning Prioritization, Execution Orchestration, Generative Quality Intelligence, and Cloud Security Monitor) coordinated through a secure multi-agent communication framework over a cloud-native microservices infrastructure. The Generative Quality Intelligence agent employs large language models to produce plain language quality narratives, defect risk summaries, and data-augmented test recommendations. The RL Prioritization agent models test selection as a Markov Decision Process, learning contextual policies from large-scale historical test execution data (47 features, rolling 36-month window). Secure cloud communication is enforced through a zero-trust API gateway with OAuth2/JWT authentication, encrypted inter-agent messaging, and multi-tenant isolation. Evaluation across 12 heterogeneous software projects over 18 months demonstrates: 88.4% test prioritization accuracy (APFD, vs. 51.2% random, 82.1% best commercial baseline); 43% test cycle time reduction; defect escape rate reduced from 8.3% to 2.1%; 340% ROI at 9-month payback. The agentic architecture scales to 50,000+ test cases with sub-400ms response time, and the generative intelligence module achieves 4.3/5.0 developer usefulness rating. AINTMA demonstrates that agentic AI, combining autonomous multi-agent coordination, generative intelligence and secure smart connectivity, can fundamentally advance software quality management in cloud-scale enterprise environments.

</details>


### [124] [Marking the Wrong Symptoms: Evaluating LLM Watermarks in Medical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20462)
*Melanie Rieff,Robin Staab,Thibaud Gloaguen,Stefan Hegselmann,Martin Vechev*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性研究了大语言模型(LLM)水印技术对医疗性能的影响，通过对5种水印方案在11个LLM和7个VLM上的测试，发现水印会导致医疗文本在词汇损坏、术语幻觉和图像发现错误归属等方面的显著退化，证明现有通用评估基准会掩盖临床关键性故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM日益整合到临床工作流程中且水印技术对追踪模型输出至关重要，但现有水印评估主要基于通用基准，忽视了医疗领域。在医疗场景中，微小的token级别扰动可能导致显著的语义变化，因此需要研究水印对医疗性能的具体影响。

Method: 研究团队构建了首个医疗领域水印影响评估框架：1) 在11个LLM和7个VLM上测试5种水印方案；2) 涵盖单模态和多模态临床推理任务；3) 引入人类专家验证的管道，系统审计医疗推理质量、术语精确度及诱导的幻觉问题。

Result: 研究发现水印会导致多种失效模式下的显著性能退化：词汇损坏、幻觉性术语生成、图像发现的错误归属或遗漏加剧。更重要的是，缺乏领域特定分析并结合聚合指标会系统性地掩盖水印诱导的实际退化，现有基准可能掩盖临床后果性故障。

Conclusion: 领域特定评估是水印模型在医疗领域安全部署的前提条件。当前通用基准可能掩盖具有临床后果的故障，因此在将水印LLM部署到医疗场景前，必须进行专门的医疗领域评估以确保安全性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly integrated into clinical workflows, stressing the need for reliable traceability of model-generated output with watermarking. Yet, most watermarks are evaluated on general-purpose benchmarks, leaving domains like medicine, where small token-level perturbations can result in significant semantic changes, underexplored. In this work, we present the first rigorous study of how LLM watermarks affect medical performance, benchmarking 5 watermarking schemes across 11 LLMs and 7 VLMs on various tasks spanning unimodal and multimodal clinical reasoning. Importantly, we complement existing evaluations by introducing a human-expert-validated pipeline for systematically auditing medical reasoning quality, terminological precision, and induced hallucinations. Our results reveal that watermarking can induce substantial degradation across multiple failure modes, including lexical corruption, hallucinated terminology, and amplified misattribution or omission of image findings. Notably, we find that the absence of domain-specific analyses, combined with aggregate metrics that miss failures inherent to clinical text, can systematically obscure practical watermark-induced degradations. Our findings establish domain-specific evaluation as a prerequisite for the safe deployment of watermarked models in medicine, where current benchmarks can otherwise mask clinically consequential failures.

</details>


### [125] [WaveformQA: Benchmarking LLM Temporal Reasoning on Digital Waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20638)
*Yichuan Liu,Daniel Cummings,Nick Vadlamudi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了WaveformQA，一个用于评估大语言模型在数字波形时序推理能力的开源问答基准，包含360个问题。评估表明LLM在简单查询上表现良好，但在复杂时序和多步问题上性能下降，且事件-时间JSON表示比标准VCD格式更能提升推理准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在代码生成和推理方面表现出色，但其在数字波形数据上的时序推理能力尚未得到充分探索。现有基准主要评估硬件描述语言(HDL)代码生成，波形仅作为辅助上下文，而数字波形的时序推理是设计验证中的关键瓶颈。

Method: 构建了WaveformQA基准，包含360个具有程序生成真实答案的问题，分为八个难度类别，涵盖多信号关联和事件排序等复杂问题。波形从开源设计实现中生成，确保可重现性和基于真实硬件行为。同时提出使用事件-时间JSON表示替代标准值变更转储(VCD)格式。

Result: 前沿LLM在简单查询上达到合理准确率，但由于上下文窗口限制和推理困难，在复杂时序和多步问题上性能显著下降。事件-时间JSON表示的波形比标准VCD格式能更好地提升LLM推理准确性。

Conclusion: WaveformQA成功填补了数字波形时序推理评估的空白，开源框架支持扩展新问题类别和导入新波形源，为研究人员提供了快速原型时序推理实验的平台，推动了LLM在硬件验证领域的发展。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in code generation and reasoning, yet their ability to perform temporal reasoning over digital waveform data remains largely unexplored. Although reasoning over digital waveforms is a critical bottleneck in design verification, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate hardware description language (HDL) code generation and use waveforms only as supplementary context. This paper presents WaveformQA, an open-source question-answering benchmark for evaluating LLM temporal reasoning over digital waveforms. The benchmark comprises 360 questions with programmatically generated ground truths across eight categories of varying difficulty, including questions targeting multi-signal correlation and event ordering. Waveforms are generated from open-source design implementations, ensuring reproducibility and grounding the benchmark in real hardware behavior. Evaluation of frontier LLMs reveals that while models achieve reasonable accuracy on simple queries, performance degrades due to context window limitations and reasoning difficulties on complex temporal and multi-step questions. In addition, we show that an event-time JSON representation of waveforms improves LLM reasoning accuracy versus the standardized value change dump (VCD) format. The open-source framework supports extending to new question categories and importing new waveform sources, enabling researchers to rapidly prototype temporal reasoning experiments.

</details>


### [126] [Workload-Aware Caching for Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20495)
*Anas Mohamed,Kaizan Haque,Azal Ahmad Khan,Chetan Sharma,Shuwen Ge,Ali Anwar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对多智能体系统的工作负载感知缓存替换策略，通过综合重计算成本、DAG依赖计数和智能体调用频率三个信号，在内存约束下保留最有价值的缓存条目，显著降低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体系统将复杂任务分解为智能体执行的有向无环图(DAG)，产生了跨查询缓存中间结果的自然机会。然而，现有的缓存替换策略仅基于访问历史统一对待所有缓存条目，忽略了智能体执行环境中独有的结构和工作负载信号。

Method: 提出一种工作负载感知的缓存替换策略，将重计算成本、DAG依赖计数和智能体调用频率三个信号组合成统一的评分函数，在内存约束下保留最有价值的缓存条目。

Result: 在三个跨越不同重用场景的多智能体基准测试中评估，该策略相对于无缓存基线减少了高达64.7%的延迟，相比次优有限容量基线平均降低31.1%的延迟，同时接近无界缓存的性能，并保持与或超过所有竞争性有限容量方法的准确性。

Conclusion: 工作负载感知的内容缓存与其他智能体系统优化方法（包括计划级缓存和并行智能体执行）形成互补，每种技术针对多智能体管道中不同的效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems decompose complex tasks into directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) of specialized agent executions, creating natural opportunities for caching intermediate results across queries. However, existing cache eviction policies treat all cached entries uniformly based on access history, ignoring structural and workload signals uniquely available in agentic execution environments. We present a workload-aware eviction policy that combines three signals, namely recomputation cost, DAG dependency count, and agent invocation frequency, into a unified scoring function that retains the most valuable entries under memory constraints. Evaluated across three multi-agent benchmarks spanning diverse reuse regimes, our policy reduces latency by up to 64.7% relative to the uncached baseline and achieves on average a 31.1% latency reduction over the next best finite-capacity baseline, while approaching the performance of an unbounded cache and maintaining accuracy on par with or exceeding all competing finite-capacity methods. We further show that workload-aware content caching is complementary to other agentic system optimization methods, including plan-level caching and parallel agent execution, with each technique targeting a distinct efficiency bottleneck in multi-agent pipelines.

</details>


### [127] [ClickGuard: Detecting and Spoiling Clickbait News with Informativeness Measures and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20463)
*Wojciech Michaluk,Tymoteusz Urban,Mateusz Kubita,Soveatin Kuntur,Anna Wróblewska*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于AI的浏览器扩展，通过混合机器学习架构检测点击诱饵文章，F1分数达91%，能为用户提供预警、评分、解释和文章摘要功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网上存在大量误导性的点击诱饵文章，传统检测方法效果有限，需要开发更有效的工具帮助用户识别和避免这类误导性内容。

Method: 采用混合机器学习架构，结合基于Transformer的嵌入、语言学特征和自定义"baitness"评分。评估了从传统向量化方法到大语言模型嵌入等多种自然语言处理技术，最终开发了基于XGBoost的模型。

Result: 在开放组合数据集上，模型达到91%的F1分数。浏览器扩展能够在用户访问点击诱饵文章前后提供预警，显示点击诱饵可能性的百分比评分，并基于分析指标提供解释，还能生成1-2句话的文章摘要。

Conclusion: 开发的浏览器扩展成功整合了先进的检测算法和用户友好的功能，为用户提供了一个实用的点击诱饵识别和规避工具，具有预警、评分、解释和摘要等多项实用功能。

Abstract: This paper presents an AI-driven browser extension that identifies clickbait to help users avoid misleading Internet articles. Moving beyond traditional detection, the application employs a hybrid machine learning architecture that combines transformer-based embeddings with linguistically motivated features and a custom "baitness" score. After evaluating various natural language processing techniques -- from classic vectorizers to large language model (LLM) embeddings -- an XGBoost-based model was developed that achieves an F1-score of 91% on the open combined dataset. Most importantly, the tool can warn users before and after they access a clickbait article. After opening an article, the user receives a percentage score indicating the likelihood that it is clickbait. The prediction is explained based on the analyzed metrics, including those specifically developed within the proposed system. The browser extension also provides a clickbait spoiler -- a one- to two-sentence summary of the entire article. Demo video:https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4}{https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=IJ1gkQV82C4

</details>


### [128] [CRAWO: Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20490)
*Eugênio Santos,Daniel Maia,Stefano Loss,José Manoel Silva,Aluizio Rocha Neto,Thais Batista,Everton Cavalcante,Nélio Cacho,Eduardo Nogueira,Daniel Araújo,Frederico Lopes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CRAWO框架，一种用于边缘智能环境下AI流水线自适应编排的架构，通过控制循环模型和硬件感知分配器实现了跨异构边缘基础设施的高效资源分配，在车辆监控场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能在智慧城市实时应用中至关重要，但在异构边缘基础设施上部署AI流水线面临挑战：设备能力差异大（从低功耗微控制器到配备加速器的系统），现有边缘编排平台主要关注部署自动化和基础设施管理，在动态条件下自适应资源分配方面效率低下且能力受限。

Method: 提出CRAWO架构框架：采用基于控制循环的模型，将分配智能与执行分离；包含硬件感知分配器和可插拔的多准则决策层，利用实时基础设施指标实现自适应工作负载放置；参考实现采用微服务架构，部署在轻量级Kubernetes发行版（K3s）上，使用自定义资源定义（CRD）进行领域建模，并使用专用操作器进行状态协调。

Result: 在车牌识别的车辆监控场景中评估表明，CRAWO改善了工作负载分布，并在延迟敏感环境中减少了对集中式云处理的依赖。

Conclusion: CRAWO框架成功解决了异构边缘环境下AI流水线编排的挑战，通过分离分配智能与执行、引入硬件感知和自适应机制，实现了更高效的工作负载分配，为边缘智能应用提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Edge Intelligence has emerged as a key paradigm for enabling real-time applications in smart cities by shifting computation from centralized cloud data centers to the network edge, thereby reducing latency and bandwidth consumption. However, deploying Artificial Intelligence (AI) pipelines across heterogeneous edge infrastructures remains challenging due to the wide range of device capabilities, from low-power microcontrollers to accelerator-equipped systems. Existing edge orchestration platforms primarily focus on deployment automation and infrastructure management, but these approaches are often inefficient and limit the ability to adaptively allocate resources under dynamic conditions. To tackle these issues, this paper introduces CRAWO (Custom Resources for Adaptive Workload Orchestration), an architectural framework for coordinating AI pipelines across distributed edge environments. CRAWO follows a control-loop-based model that separates allocation intelligence from execution by managing placement decisions, state management, and inter-stage data flows while instantiating services on edge nodes. The framework incorporates a hardware-aware allocator with a pluggable multi-criteria decision layer that leverages real-time infrastructure metrics to enable adaptive workload placement. The reference implementation adopts a microservices architecture deployed on a lightweight Kubernetes distribution (K3s), using Custom Resource Definitions (CRDs) for domain modeling and a dedicated operator for state reconciliation. Evaluation in a vehicle surveillance scenario using license plate recognition demonstrates improved workload distribution and reduced reliance on centralized cloud processing in latency-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [129] [MKEvolve: A Modular Multi-Agent Framework for Kernel Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.20501)
*Jason Yoo,Rajarshi Saha,Shaowei Zhu,Tao Yu,Wei Tang,Youngsuk Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MKEvolve框架，通过模块化分解和迭代协同进化的方法，解决了LLM生成硬件加速器内核时的正确性和性能问题，在减少token使用的同时提升了内核质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的代码生成进展迅速，但为硬件加速器编写正确且高性能的内核仍然是扩展现代机器学习工作负载的关键瓶颈。现有的端到端直接合成方法在处理复杂PyTorch模块时面临正确性和性能挑战。

Method: 提出MKEvolve（模块化内核进化）框架，采用迭代协同进化策略：1)将复杂PyTorch模块进行模块化分解；2)为每个子模块生成LLM内核；3)通过跨迭代的拆分和融合操作细化分解；4)对每个子内核独立进行LLM驱动的束搜索优化。最终内核是独立验证的子内核的程序化组合。
